Implement:
```
def grad_q_smooth(h, p, n):
    dx=2/n
    mid=n-1
    A=np.zeros(n); A[1:]=p[mid+1:]
    B=np.zeros(n); B[1:]=p[:mid][::-1]
    p0=p[mid]
    # prefix sums
    prefA=np.empty(n); np.cumsum(A, out=prefA)
    P1=prefA[n-1::-1] # P1_i=sum d=0..n-1-i A[d]
    # want d=1.. so subtract A0=0; ok
    prefB=np.empty(n); np.cumsum(B, out=prefB)
    B1=prefB
    Z=A+1j*B
    if n small direct? For packing complex direct not; use fft always? n=400 maybe fft ok. But direct four conv maybe 4*160k=640k, fine for 1000 =640M maybe ok C? np.convolve in C fast.
```

Maybe implement FFT packing for better scalability. Need next_fast_len from scipy.fft? Could import scipy.fft as sfft and use sfft.next_fast_len. But to reduce dependency, use simple power of two: `N = 1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()`. Then:
```
def conv_full_packed(h, Z, n):
    N=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    a=np.zeros(N, complex); a[:n]=h
    b=np.zeros(N, complex); b[:n]=Z
    return sfft.irfft(sfft.rfft(a)*sfft.rfft(b), N)[:2*n-1]
```
This uses complex FFT each gradient; overhead maybe higher than direct for n=400. Could choose threshold: if n <= 512 direct np.convolve(h, Z) else FFT. But direct complex conv maybe okay. We can implement both with threshold `n > 600`.

Gradient:
```
back = np.convolve(h, Z, 'full') # length 2n-1; index i 0..n-1
fwd = np.convolve(h[::-1], Z, 'full') # index n-1-i
FA = fwd[n-1::-1].real
BA = back[:n].real
FB = fwd[n-1::-1].imag
BB = back[:n].imag
g = p0*(1-2*h) + (P1 - BA - FA) + (B1 - BB - FB)
```

Let's formalize binary state and evaluation.

State:
- n even integer.
- fullmask = (1<<n)-1.
- valid_masks[d] for d=1..n-1: (1 << (n-d)) - 1. Good.
- ones_mask int.
- zeros_mask = fullmask ^ ones_mask.
- max_val maybe C count not scaled.

Function eval_mask(n, mask): returns max_count and maybe lag/count details. For local search, need max_count. Use loop d from 1 to n-1. We can also early stop if max_count reaches upper bound? For optimization, need full max. But we can limit? Use full.

Potential optimization: Precompute for each d: `valid_masks[d]`, `shifted?` Not enough.

Evaluation function:
```
def binary_eval(mask, n, fullmask, masks):
    zos = fullmask ^ mask
    best = 0
    for d in range(1,n):
        s = mask >> d
        m = masks[d]
        a = (mask & (m ^ s)).bit_count()? Since s only low bits? s & m. Need zero at i+d: (m & ~s) but m restricts. Use m & (m ^ s) because bits 1 in m and 0 in s. Since s may have bits beyond? m ensures low.
        b = (zos & s & m).bit_count()
        if a>best: best=a
        if b>best: best=b
        if best == n//2? cannot less? no early.
    return best
```
Use `m & (m ^ s)` works because within m, if s bit 0 then m^s has 1, if s bit 1 then 0. Good faster than ~ maybe.
`b = (zos & s & m).bit_count()`. Could also `s & (m ^ mask?)` maybe zos. But zos computed.
Could combine? max of a,b. Note `a+b = popcount(m ^ s & m)`? Actually D = popcount(m & (mask ^ (mask>>d)))? yes.

Time: Python loop 399, each several big int ops. Should be okay. For n=800, 799 loops, double.

Local search:
- Start from seed; compute max_count.
- While time < deadline:
   - Generate candidate swaps.
   - For each candidate, compute new mask = mask ^ (1<<i) ^ (1<<j), evaluate, if max_count < best accepted; stop moving to new accepted; if not, keep first few neutral? To escape local, use simulated annealing: allow increases with probability based on delta and temperature; or store neutral moves.
   - To avoid high cost, candidate count limited; after T no improvement, reheat/random restart.

Candidate generation strategies:
1. Violation-based: For current mask, find active lags and bad positions. Need compute not just max_count but for some lags, positions. Then propose swaps involving a 1 and 0 from same bad pair.
   - During eval, track best_lags where count >= best - 2 maybe. But storing positions for each lag expensive. Instead after eval, get top lags (e.g. 10) by recomputing counts for those d. Determine for a top lag d, the offending direction plus/minus. If plus count high, choose positions i in plus violations: mask bit i=1 and mask bit i+d=0. A direct swap i with i+d reduces that plus count by 1 but increases that minus by 1. If minus below plus, max may drop. This is a natural local move. Collect pairs (i, i+d) from a sample of violations (e.g. first 50) and also random sample.
   - Also choose swaps where both positions involved in different high lags? Too complex.
2. Random 1/0 swaps: choose random i with 1 and j with 0.
3. Nearby swaps: choose random 1 and a random nearby 0? maybe.

At each accept, recompute top lags? Could be costly. Simpler: Maintain current best. To propose violations, we can do a lightweight sweep to find lags with max counts and collect some indices. But collecting indices requires scanning bits. For a top lag, if a large, need sample positions where mask bit i=1 and mask>>d bit i=0. Generate candidate list:
- From top active lags (topm maybe 8 with count close to best): for count a plus violations, add i, i+d if i bit one; for count b minus violations, add i, i+d if i zero? For b, `bad = zeros & (mask>>d) & m` bits i where h_i=0 and h_{i+d}=1; swap i (0) with i+d (1) -> same pair reversed. So pair (i, i+d).

Gradient formula with w probs:
```
def smooth_grad_from_w(h, w, n, dx):
    mid=n-1
    A=np.zeros(n); A[1:]=w[mid+1:]
    B=np.zeros(n); B[1:]=w[:mid][::-1]
    p0=w[mid]
    Z=A+1j*B
    # use direct convolve if n<=600 else FFT? Let's choose direct np.convolve for simplicity and reliability.
    back=np.convolve(h, Z, mode='full')
    fwd=np.convolve(h[::-1], Z, mode='full')
    prefA=np.cumsum(A); P1=prefA[n-1::-1]
    prefB=np.cumsum(B); B1=prefB
    BA=back[:n].real; BB=back[:n].imag
    FA=fwd[n-1::-1].real; FB=fwd[n-1::-1].imag
    g = p0*(1-2*h) + (P1 - BA - FA) + (B1 - BB - FB)
    return g*dx
```
Need ensure `fwd` length 2n-1; index n-1::-1 gives array length n. `fwd[n-1::-1]` correct. In Numpy, slice stop default down to step -1. Good.

```
def conv_full_pack(h, Z, n):
    if n <= 700:
        return np.convolve(h, Z, mode='full')
    m = 2*n - 1
    N = 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    a = np.zeros(N, dtype=complex); a[:n] = h
    b = np.zeros(N, dtype=complex); b[:n] = Z
    spec = np.fft.rfft(a) * np.fft.rfft(b)
    out = np.fft.irfft(spec, N)
    return out[:m]
```
This gives linear conv if zero padded. Good. For n small direct. We'll use it twice per gradient: back with h, fwd with h[::-1]. Potential memory okay.

def binary_local_search(mask, n, deadline, seed, max_evals maybe, time_limit):
    fullmask=(1<<n)-1
    masks = [(1 << (n-d)) - 1 for d in range(1,n)] # list length n-1; for d index d-1.
    best = binary_eval(...)
    rng=RandomState(seed)
    t_start=time.time()

       candidates = generate_candidates(mask,n,fullmask,masks,best,rng)
       best_cand=None; best_val=best
       for (i,j) in candidates:
           if (mask>>(i)).bit... ensure one zero.
           new=mask ^ (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
           val=binary_eval(new,n,fullmask,masks)
           if val<best_cand: best_cand=(new,val)
       if best_cand and best_cand[1]<best:
          mask,best=best_cand[0],best_cand[1]
          no_improve=0
       else:
          # maybe simulate anneal: if time and no_improve<... pick random candidate with val <= best + temp
          ...
          no_improve += len(candidates)
          if no_improve > NUM_STUCK: random restart from best mask with random perturbation?

def binary_top_pairs(mask,n,fullmask,masks,best,rng,max_pairs=60):
   zos=fullmask^mask
   pairs=[]
   top_counts=[] maybe list (count, d, type, bad)
   for d in 1..n-1:

def binary_sa(mask,n,..., deadline, seed, min_deadline):

    global_mask = mask

           else if val <= cur_val + 2 and rng.rand() < math.exp(-(val-cur_val)/max(temp,1e-3)):

```
def make_active_weights(h, n, eps):
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')*(2/n)
    C=c.max()
    active=c > C - eps
    w=np.zeros_like(c)
    if not active.any(): active[np.argmax(c)]=True
    w[active]=1/active.sum()
    # optionally add small mass to all lags to avoid ignoring: w = (1-0.1)*active uniform + 0.1/len(c)
    return w
```

### Validate gradient formula with numpy correlate

Need ensure `smooth_grad_from_w` matches c indexing. Let's test mentally with small random h/n=3 maybe finite difference. For n=3, mid=2.
Indices: p0 lag -2: q_-2 = h2(1-h0).
p1 lag -1: h1(1-h0)
p2 lag 0: h0(1-h0)+h1(1-h1)+h2(1-h2)
p3 lag +1: h0(1-h1)
p4 lag +2: h0(1-h2)
Our A[1]=p3, A[2]=p4. B[1]=p1, B[2]=p0.
Gradient for h_i:
Let's test i=0:
A contribution: P1_0 = sum d=1..2 A = p3+p4. forward A: p3 h1 + p4 h2. backward A:0. => (p3+p4) - p3 h1 - p4 h2.
B: B1_0=0. forward B: p1 h1 + p0 h2. backward 0. => -p1 h1 - p0 h2.
p0 term: p2(1-2h0).
Total: p2 -2p2h0 + p3(1-h1)+p4(1-h2) -p1h1 -p0h2.
Actual:
q0: h0-h0^2 => grad 1-2h0 => p2
q+1: h0(1-h1) => p3(1-h1)
q+2: h0(1-h2) => p4(1-h2)
q-1: h1(1-h0) => wrt h0 -p1 h1
q-2: h2(1-h0) => -p0 h2 correct.
Good.

Thus gradient correct.

### Potential bug in binary bit mask ordering and C evaluation

Our bit mask bit i corresponds to h[i]. In eval, `s = mask >> d` makes bit i old i+d. Low mask length n-d. qplus = bits i where h_i=1 and s_i=0 => old i+d=0. qminus = bits i where h_i=0 and s_i=1. This matches q+d and q-d for d positive. Max count. Good.

Convert mask to h: index i as bit i. np.correlate uses same order. Good.

Potential issue: L-BFGS from binary with high beta may get stuck at binary (since local min?). Weighted active may adjust fractional. Use lower beta first to smooth, then high.

### New idea: use Lagrangian over active lags to equalize? Active weights with L-BFGS can equalize. Could also run multiple active polishes with different random perturbations. We'll implement `active_polish` that loops eps and maybe small perturbations if stuck.

```
def active_polish(h, n, deadline, seed):
   h=project_box_sum(h,n/2); best=erdos_c5
   rng=RandomState(seed)
   eps_list=[1e-2,5e-3,2e-3,1e-3,5e-4,2e-4,1e-4,5e-5]
   for eps in eps_list:
      if time>deadline-10: break
      # compute weights; optionally random perturb if not improving last
      for it in range(2):
        w=make_active_weights(h,eps)
        cand=weighted_lbfgs(h,w,mx,time)
        c=erdos_c5(...); if c<best-1e-12: update
      if time>...
   return h,best
```

`project_box_sum` from previous but maybe shorter:
```
def project_box_sum(x,target):
    n=x.size; y=np.clip(x,0,1)
    if abs(y.sum()-target)<1e-12: return y
    lo=np.min(x)-1; hi=np.max(x)+1
    for _ in range(60): ...
    y=np.clip(x-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    diff=target-y.sum()
    for _ in range(3):
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       mask=(y>1e-12)&(y<1-1e-12)
       if mask.any(): y[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
       else: y+=diff/n
       y=np.clip(y,0,1)
       diff=target-y.sum()
    return y
```
Fine.

`conv_full_pack`: threshold 256; use FFT full complex. Need import numpy.fft? `np.fft` available. 
```
def conv_full_pack(a, z, n):
    m=2*n-1
    if n <= 256:
        return np.convolve(a, z, mode='full')
    N=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    aa=np.empty(N, complex); aa.fill(0); aa[:n]=a
    bb=np.empty(N, complex); bb.fill(0); bb[:n]=z
    out=np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(aa)*np.fft.fft(bb))[:m]
    return out
```
`a` may be h[::-1] view; assign to complex. Good.

`smooth_grad_from_w` uses conv_full_pack twice. Need h real, Z complex. For `h[::-1]`, pass view; in `conv_full_pack`, `aa[:n]=a` handles.
```
def smooth_grad_from_w(h,w,n,dx):
    mid=n-1
    A=np.zeros(n); A[1:]=w[mid+1:]
    B=np.zeros(n); B[1:]=w[:mid][::-1]
    Z=A+1j*B
    back=conv_full_pack(h,Z,n)
    fwd=conv_full_pack(h[::-1],Z,n)
    prefA=np.cumsum(A); prefB=np.cumsum(B)
    g = w[mid]*(1-2*h) + (prefA[n-1::-1] - back[:n].real - fwd[n-1::-1].real) + (prefB - back[:n].imag - fwd[n-1::-1].imag)
    return g*dx
```
Check `prefA` length n; `prefA[n-1::-1]` length n. `prefB` length n. Good.

Potential memory: `fwd[n-1::-1]` each twice; can store `fwd_rev = fwd[n-1::-1]`. But okay.

`compute_smooth(x)`:
```
st=SM; n=st['n']; dx=st['dx']; beta=st['beta']
c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')*dx
f,w=softmax_values(c,beta)
g=smooth_grad_from_w(x,w,n,dx)
res=x.sum()-st['target']; rho=st['rho']
if rho: f += 0.5*rho*res*res; g += rho*res
st['cx']=x.copy(); st['cg']=g; st['cf']=f; return f
```
No need lam.

But for L-BFGS-B, penalty rho with large value may dominate; choose `rho=1e3` maybe. Since sum initially exact. If sum drifts, g constant. For beta low, S gradient small; penalty important. `rho=1e4`. Could adapt? We'll pass? `refine_smooth` set `SM['rho']=1e4`.

`softmax_values`: if beta large, compute `z=beta*(c-maxc)`, underflow. stable.
```
def softmax_values(c,beta):
    maxc=float(np.max(c)); z=beta*(c-maxc); z=np.minimum(z,0.0); ew=np.exp(z); s=ew.sum()
    f=maxc+np.log(s)/beta
    w=ew/s if s>0 else onehot
    return f,w
```
For beta=0? not.

`s_jac`:
```
def s_jac(x):
  st=SM
  if st.get('cx') is not None and st.get('cf') is not None and np.array_equal(x, st['cx']): return st['cg'].copy()
  compute_smooth(x); return st['cg'].copy()
```
`ldots`.

`lbfgs_optimize`:
```
def lbfgs_optimize(fun,jac,h,maxiter,deadline):
    n=len(h); target=0.5*n
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem<5: return project_box_sum(h,target)
    mx=int(min(maxiter, max(15, int(rem/2.0))))
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res=so.minimize(fun,h,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=jac,bounds=bounds,
                       options={'maxiter':mx,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':40,'disp':False})
       return project_box_sum(res.x,n_target)
    except: return project_box_sum(h,target)
```
Need `target` set in state before call. In `refine_smooth`, set state.

`refine_smooth(h,beta,maxiter,deadline)`:

def refine_smooth(h,beta,maxiter,deadline):
   n=len(h); SM.update({'n':n,'dx':2/n,'target':0.5*n,'beta':float(beta),'rho':1e4}); ... clear cache
   cand=lbfgs_optimize(s_fun,s_jac,h,maxiter,deadline)
   return cand, erdos_c5(cand,n)
```
Update dict method? Since top-level dict mutable. Use assignments.

`active_polish`:
```
def active_polish(h,deadline,seed):
   n=len(h); h=project; best=erdos_c5
   eps=[1e-2,5e-3,2e-3,1e-3,5e-4,2e-4,1e-4,5e-5]
   for eps in eps:
      if time.time()>deadline-10: break
      for _ in range(2):
         if time.time()>deadline-10: break
         w=make_active_weights(h,n,eps)
         mx=40 if n<=500 else 25
         cand,c=refine_weighted(h,w,mx,deadline)
         if c<best-1e-13: h=cand; best=c
   return h,best
```

`rotation_binary(n, alpha, beta)`:
```
i=np.arange(n)
ang=(i*alpha+beta)%1.0
arr=ang<0.5
diff=n//2 - int(arr.sum())
if diff:
    # distance to boundary of interval [0,0.5)
    dist=np.empty(n)
    ones=arr
    dist[ones]=np.minimum(ang[ones], 0.5-ang[ones])
    zeros=~ones
    dist[zeros]=np.minimum(1-ang[zeros], ang[zeros]-0.5)
    cand_idx=np.flatnonzero(zeros if diff>0 else ones)
    if diff>0:
        order=np.argsort(dist[cand_idx])[:diff]
        arr[cand_idx[order]]=True
    else:
        order=np.argsort(dist[cand_idx])[:(-diff)]
        arr[cand_idx[order]]=False
return bits_from_bool(arr)
```
Need ensure `dist[zeros]`: for ang in [0.5,1): min(ang-0.5, 1-ang). Good.

`binary_eval(mask,n,fullmask,masks)`:
Precompute masks list length n-1. For d=1, index0. 
```
def binary_eval(mask,n,fullmask,masks):
    zos=fullmask ^ mask
    best=0
    for d in range(1,n):
        s=mask >> d
        m=masks[d-1]
        a=(mask & (m ^ s)).bit_count()
        b=(zos & s & m).bit_count()
        if a>best: best=a
        if b>best: best=b
    return best
```
Maybe use local variables for speed. Could precompute `n_range=range(1,n)`. Not.

`binary_top_pairs(mask,n,fullmask,masks,current_best,rng,max_pairs=50)`:
Need sample from violations near best. We'll compute counts and store top bad masks? To avoid reconstruct bad for sampled lags, we can store `(d, val, bad)` for selected? But bad is int. If top_counts large, storing all bad for near best may memory but n=400, okay. Simpler:
```
def binary_top_pairs(..., max_pairs=60):
    zos=fullmask ^ mask
    top=[]
    for d in range(1,n):
       s=mask>>d; m=masks[d-1]
       a=...; b=...
       if a >= current_best-2:
          if a>0: top.append((a,d, mask & (m ^ s)))
       if b >= current_best-2:
          if b>0: top.append((b,d, zos & s & m))
    if not top:
       # find absolute max and add
       best=0; bestd=0; bad=0
       ... loop again
       top.append((best,bestd,bad))
    top.sort(reverse=True) # by count
    top=top[:8]
    pairs=[]
    for cnt,d,bad in top:
       x=bad
       while x and len(pairs)<max_pairs:
          lsb=x & -x; i=lsb.bit_length()-1
          j=i+d
          if (j<n) and i!=j:
             pairs.append((i,j))
          x ^= lsb
       if len(pairs)>=max_pairs: break
    # random valid swaps
    attempts=0
    while len(pairs)<max_pairs and attempts<max_pairs*3:
       attempts+=1
       i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
       if i!=j:
          bi=(mask>>i)&1; bj=(mask>>j)&1
          if bi!=bj: pairs.append((i,j))
    # dedup
    return list(dict.fromkeys(pairs))[:max_pairs]
```
This loops over all lags plus set bits. Good.

`binary_local_search(mask,n,fullmask,masks,deadline,seed)`:
```
def binary_local_search(start_mask,n,fullmask,masks,deadline,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    cur_mask=start_mask; cur_val=binary_eval(...)
    best_mask=cur_mask; best_val=cur_val
    temp=1.6
    stuck=0
    while time.time()<deadline-2:
       cands=binary_top_pairs(cur_mask,n,fullmask,masks,cur_val,rng,40)
       if not cands: break
       accepted=False
       for (i,j) in cands:
           if time.time()>deadline-2: break
           if i==j: continue
           bi=(cur_mask>>i)&1; bj=(cur_mask>>j)&1
           if bi==bj: continue
           nm=cur_mask ^ (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
           val=binary_eval(nm,n,fullmask,masks)
           if val < cur_val:
               cur_mask=nm; cur_val=val; accepted=True; temp=min(1.6,temp*1.05?)
               if val<best_val: best_val=val; best_mask=nm
               break
           elif val <= cur_val + 2:
               if rng.rand() < math.exp(-(val-cur_val)/max(temp,1e-6)):
                   cur_mask=nm; cur_val=val; accepted=True
                   break
       if not accepted:
           temp*=0.97
           stuck+=1
           if stuck>20:
              # perturb current: perform 3 random valid swaps without eval? then reevaluate
              for _ in range(3):
                 # find valid random
                 while True: i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if bits diff break
                 cur_mask ^= ...
              cur_val=binary_eval(cur_mask,...)
              # maybe if worse than best, revert? Keep current as anneal; but global best separate.
              temp=1.2; stuck=0
       else:
           stuck=max(0,stuck-1)
       # if best_val very low maybe break? If best_val*2/n <=0.380 and maybe continue to improve? break? We'll let run timeout.
    return best_mask,best_val
```
Anneal current may wander far and become bad; but candidate gen based on current, and global best kept. It might not return to good basin. We can also if cur_val > best_val + 5, reset cur_mask=best_mask. Good.
```
if cur_val > best_val + 5: cur_mask=best_mask; cur_val=best_val; temp=1.2
```
This prevents lost.

Candidate count 40 may evaluate many. If no accepted, random perturb.

Need ensure time spent in `binary_top_pairs` (full eval) plus candidate evals. `binary_top_pairs` loops full n and set bits. Good.

`mask_to_best_h` after binary_run:
```
h=mask_to_array(best_mask,n); c=erdos_c5(h,n)
```

### Resampling n choices

`resample_n(h,n,nh)` from previous: linear interp and project. Need ensure n even? If target n even. Use nh even.
```
def resample_n(h,n,nh):
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n); y=np.linspace(0,2,nh)
   hh=np.interp(y,x,h)
   return project_box_sum(hh,0.5*nh)
```

### Final schedule code

Let's craft run carefully to avoid too much time.

Pseudo:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); total=max(10.0, float(budget_s)-5.0); deadline=t0+total
    # load/initialize best
    try: h=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n=int(len(h))
    except: n=400; h=make_rotation_h(n, 1/80, 0.3)
    if n<2: n=400; h=make_rotation_h(...)
    if n>900: # resample down
        nh=800 if n>800 else n
        if nh%2: nh-=1
        h=resample_n(h,n,nh); n=nh
    if n%2: n-=1; h=resample? if odd, maybe take first n? But assume even. If odd, project? Need n even for binary. Continuous can odd but binary half? Problem n can be any; sum n/2 may non-integer if n odd? Constraint sum(h)=n/2 exactly means if n odd sum non-integer possible with fractional values; yes n odd allowed continuous. But binary requires even. Parent likely 400 even. We can allow odd for continuous; binary use n_bin=n if even else n-1.
    h=project_box_sum(h,0.5*n)
    best_h=h; best_n=n; best_c=erdos_c5(best_h,best_n)

    # continuous early
    end1=t0+0.18*total
    for beta,mx in [(50,60),(300,80),(2000,70),(20000,60),(200000,50)]:
        if time.time()>end1-8: break
        cand,c=refine_smooth(best_h,beta,mx if best_n>600 else mx*2, deadline)
        if c<best_c-1e-14: best_h,c=...
    if time.time()<end1:
        cand,c=active_polish(best_h,end1,seed+5); update

    # binary at current n
    n_bin=best_n if best_n%2==0 else best_n-1
    if n_bin>=50:
       # ensure base_h at n_bin
       base_h=best_h if best_n==n_bin else resample_n(best_h,best_n,n_bin)
       end_bin=t0+0.55*total
       hbin,cbin=binary_run_n(n_bin, base_h, end_bin, seed+11)
       if cbin<best_c: update best_h,best_n
       # if binary improved less as h separate? We'll also maybe keep binary best_h.
       # If current best still from n_bin, continue.

    # continuous polish best (maybe from binary)
    end_cont2=t0+0.72*total
    # If best_n not even, continuous ok.
    cand,c=refine_smooth(best_h, 50000, 50, end_cont2); update
    cand,c=active_polish(best_h, end_cont2, seed+23); update

    # second n exploration
    end2=t0+0.88*total
    # choose target_n different: if best_n==400, use 300? plus 600? maybe 300 faster.
    opts=[]
    if best_n != 300: opts.append(300)
    if best_n != 600: opts.append(600)
    if best_n != 500: opts.append(500)
    # choose one or two based on time. Use smaller 300 first, then 500/600 if time.
    for tn in opts[:2]:
       if time.time()>end2-30: break
       base_h=resample_n(best_h,best_n,tn)
       if tn%2: tn-=1; base_h=resample... (but opts even)
       # binary search with shorter and continuous quick
       h_t,c_t=binary_run_n(tn, base_h, min(end2-15, t+ (end2-time)*0.5), seed+tn)
       if c_t<best_c: update
       # continuous refine at tn if best_n==tn or even with short
       if time.time()<end2-15:
           cand,c=refine_smooth(h_t if best_n==tn else best_h, 50000, 30, end2)
           if best_n==tn and c<best_c-1e-14? Actually refine h_t, if c < best_c update best_n=tn.
    # final polish
    end_final=deadline-8
    if time.time()<end_final-20:
       cand,c=refine_smooth(best_h,500000,40,end_final); update
    if time.time()<end_final:
       cand,c=active_polish(best_h,end_final,seed+55); update
    best_h=project_box_sum(best_h,0.5*best_n)
    best_c=erdos_c5(best_h,best_n)
    # maybe try evaluate with evaluate_erdos_solution? optional
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

Need be mindful: `binary_run_n` expects deadline absolute. If end_bin maybe 55% total ~600s; large. Continuous early 18% ~200s. 1100s total => end1=198s, end_bin=605s, end_cont2=792s, end2=968s, final 1092s. This gives binary 400s. Good. But continuous early 200s may be too long? Previous parent already optimized; maybe 100s enough. Use fractions: 0.12 (130s) for initial continuous, binary 0.5 (545s), continuous 0.15 (165s), second n 0.15 (165s), final 0.06. Let's set:
- end1 = t0 + 0.12*total
- end_bin = t0 + 0.52*total
- end_cont2 = t0 + 0.68*total
- end2 = t0 + 0.88*total
- end_final = deadline - 8

But if no parent (initial missing), initial continuous from rotation maybe needs more. Still okay.

Need ensure not overrun in binary_run_n with many seeds. It gets deadline end_bin (absolute). It will stop before end_bin-10.

### Potential problem: L-BFGS with penalty and beta very high may fail due objective noisy? Objective smooth but sharp; L-BFGS line search may take many evals. Limit maxiter based on time. In `lbfgs_optimize`, mx = min(maxiter, int(rem/2)). For beta high, one gradient maybe ~0.2ms? Actually compute c correlate O(n^2) + conv. For n=400, 1ms; 2 =2ms. 60 iter=0.12s. Many calls. Time fine.

But `np.correlate` direct O(n^2) for n=600 maybe 360k, ok. For n=800 640k, ok.

```
import time
import math
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as so

SM = {'n':0, 'dx':1.0, 'target':0.0, 'beta':1.0,'rho':1e4,'cx':None,'cg':None,'cf':None}
AW = {...}
```

`softmax_values`:
```
def softmax_values(c, beta):
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    z = beta * (c - maxc)
    np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0, out=z)
    ew = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ew.sum())
    f = maxc + math.log(s) / beta if s>0 else maxc
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else: w = ew / s
    return f, w
```
If c length? no.

`compute_smooth`:
```
def compute_smooth(x):
    st=SM
    n=int(st['n']); dx=float(st['dx'])
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full') * dx
    f,w = softmax_values(c, float(st['beta']))
    g = smooth_grad_from_w(x,w,n,dx)
    res = float(x.sum() - st['target'])
    rho = float(st['rho'])
    if rho:
      f += 0.5*rho*res*res
      g = g + rho*res
    st['cx']=x.copy(); st['cf']=f; st['cg']=g
    return f
```
If n=0? no.

`compute_weighted` similar; `w=AW['w']` fixed; compute `c`; `f=float(np.dot(w,c))`; `g=smooth_grad_from_w(x,w,n,dx)`.

`s_fun`:
```
def s_fun(x): return compute_smooth(x)
```
`compute_smooth` returns f and sets cache. `s_jac` uses cache. Need if `SM['cx'] is None`: compute.
```
def s_jac(x):
    st=SM
    if st['cx'] is not None and np.array_equal(x, st['cx']): return st['cg'].copy()
    compute_smooth(x); return st['cg'].copy()
```
`aw_jac` similarly.

`lbfgs_optimize` uses `fun(jac)`. It doesn't know state target? project target uses len. Good.

Potential issue: L-BFGS-B may call `fun` with x outside bounds due FP? Bounds enforced. Fine.

`refine_smooth` sets SM state. Need clear cache. `SM['cx']=None` etc.
```
def refine_smooth(h,beta,maxiter,deadline):
    h=np.asarray(h,float); n=len(h)
    SM['n']=n; SM['dx']=2.0/n; SM['target']=0.5*n; SM['beta']=float(beta); SM['rho']=1e4; SM['cx']=None; SM['cg']=None
    cand=lbfgs_optimize(s_fun,s_jac,h,maxiter,deadline)
    return cand, erdos_c5(cand,n)
```

`refine_weighted` similar with AW.

- In `binary_top_pairs`, if `not top`, loop again to find max. The `bad` computed. Good.

Let's detail `binary_top_pairs`:
```
def binary_top_pairs(mask,n,fullmask,masks,current_best,rng,max_pairs=48):
    zos = fullmask ^ mask
    top=[]
    for d in range(1,n):
        s = mask >> d
        m = masks[d-1]
        a = (mask & (m ^ s)).bit_count()
        b = (zos & s & m).bit_count()
        if a >= current_best - 2 and a > 0:
            top.append((a,d, mask & (m ^ s) & m))
        if b >= current_best - 2 and b > 0:
            top.append((b,d, zos & s & m))
    if not top:
        best=0; bestd=1; bad=0
        for d in range(1,n):
            s=mask>>d; m=masks[d-1]
            a=...; b=...
            if a>best: best=a; bestd=d; bad=mask & (m ^ s) & m
            if b>best: best=b; bestd=d; bad=zos & s & m
        if best>0: top.append((best,bestd,bad))
    top.sort(reverse=True)
    top = top[:8]
    pairs=[]
    for cnt,d,bad in top:
        x=bad
        while x and len(pairs)<max_pairs:
            lsb=x & -x
            i=lsb.bit_length()-1
            j=i+d
            if 0 <= j < n and i != j:
                pairs.append((i,j))
            x ^= lsb
    attempts=0
    while len(pairs)<max_pairs and attempts < max_pairs*4:
        attempts += 1
        i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
        if i != j:
            bi=(mask>>i)&1; bj=(mask>>j)&1
            if bi != bj:
                pairs.append((i,j))
    if pairs:
        pairs = list(dict.fromkeys(pairs))[:max_pairs]
    return pairs
```
Potential: `current_best` int; `current_best-2` might be negative, then many top. OK.

`binary_eval` could be integrated in top_pairs to avoid double eval. In local search, we call current eval at start, then top_pairs recomputes. After accepting, current_val known from candidate eval. We don't call eval again until next top_pairs. Good.

`binary_local_search` candidate generation after current accepted: `cands = binary_top_pairs(cur_mask,...,cur_val,rng,40)`. It recomputes all lags. Good. For each candidate, eval candidate. If accepted improving, we break and next round. If no, after trying all candidates, SA. Could be expensive if 40 candidates and no accept; but top_pairs overhead once. Good.

If candidates list includes invalid after cur_bit changed? cur_mask hasn't changed during candidate loop (we break after accept), so valid.

In SA, if accepted increase, we update cur_val but not global; if val < best_val update. Good.

Temperature: initial maybe 1.0? For integer max, accept +1 with prob e^-1=0.37. +2 0.13. Good. Decrease 0.98 each no accept. If accept, maybe keep or increase slightly. Reset if reset.

### Binary seed time allocation

```
def binary_run_n(n, base_h, deadline, seed):
    fullmask=(1<<n)-1
    masks=[(1 << (n-d)) - 1 for d in range(1,n)]
    seeds=generate_binary_seeds(n,base_h,seed)
    scored=[]
    for m in seeds:
       v=binary_eval(m,n,fullmask,masks)
       scored.append((v,m))
    scored.sort()
    best_val, best_mask = scored[0]
    idx=0
    while idx < len(scored) and time.time() < deadline - 15:
       val,m = scored[idx]
       remaining_seeds = len(scored)-idx
       share = (deadline - time.time() - 10) / max(1, remaining_seeds)
       sub_deadline = time.time() + max(5.0, min(45.0, share))
       if sub_deadline > deadline - 10: sub_deadline = deadline - 10
       bm,bv = binary_local_search(m,n,fullmask,masks,sub_deadline, seed+idx)
       if bv < best_val: best_val=bv; best_mask=bm
       idx+=1
       if best_val * 2.0 / n <= 0.379 and remaining_seeds > 10:
           # may skip lower-quality seeds? But if already <0.38, continue to improve. Could break after a few? To save time for continuous, if best <=0.375 break.
           if best_val * 2.0 / n <= 0.375 and time.time() < deadline - 60: break
    h=mask_to_array(best_mask,n); c=erdos_c5(h,n); return h,c
```
If best C=0.38, share schedule continues maybe. Could break if `best_val * 2/n <=0.375` only. That's unrealistic. If 0.38, continue.

But `binary_local_search` for each seed may not improve from initial; if initial high, 45s wasted. Sorted seeds low initial, good.

`generate_binary_seeds`: For n=300, alpha 1/k cycles. Use loop. Need not too many seeds. Let's generate maybe 14.

```
def generate_binary_seeds(n, base_h, seed):
    n=int(n)
    if len(base_h) != n: base_h = resample_n(base_h, len(base_h), n)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(int(seed)+101)
    seeds=[]
    seeds.append(binary_from_h(base_h,n))
    # deterministic rotations:
    for k in (40,64,96,128,192):
       alpha=1.0/k
       for beta in (0.21,0.57):
           seeds.append(rotation_binary(n,alpha,beta))
    # random rotations
    for _ in range(4):
       k=int(rng.randint(24,220))
       beta=float(rng.rand())
       seeds.append(rotation_binary(n,1.0/k,beta))
    # random balanced
    for _ in range(3):
       idx=rng.choice(n,size=n//2,replace=False)
       arr=np.zeros(n,bool); arr[idx]=True; seeds.append(bits_from_bool(arr))
    # dedup
    seen=set(); uniq=[]
    for m in seeds:
       if m not in seen: seen.add(m); uniq.append(m)
    return uniq
```
1 +10 +4+3=18. Good. For n even. If n small e.g. 50, random choice OK.

def continuous_seed_search(n, base_h, deadline, seed):
    best_h=np.asarray(base_h,float)
    best_c=erdos_c5(best_h,n)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(int(seed)+17)
    # fixed rotations
    for k in (64,96,128,192):
       for beta in (0.15,0.45,0.75):
           if time.time() > deadline - 25: return best_h,best_c
           hseed=make_rotation_h(n,1.0/k,beta)
           cand,c=refine_smooth(hseed, 500.0, 25, deadline)
           if c<best_c-1e-14: best_h=cand; best_c=c
    for _ in range(6):
       if time.time() > deadline - 25: break
       k=int(rng.randint(32,240)); beta=float(rng.rand())
       hseed=make_rotation_h(n,1.0/k,beta)
       cand,c=refine_smooth(hseed, 1000.0, 25, deadline)
       if c<best_c-1e-14: update
    return best_h,best_c
```

In `erdos_c5`, if h length not n? Use len.
```
def erdos_c5(h,n):
    n=int(n)
    dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*dx)
```
If h length n; okay.

`project_box_sum`:
```
def project_box_sum(x,target):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    n=int(x.size)
    if n==0: return x
    y=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    if abs(float(y.sum())-float(target)) < 1e-13:
        return y
    lo=float(np.min(x))-1.0
    hi=float(np.max(x))+1.0
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       y=np.clip(x-mid,0.0,1.0)
       if float(y.sum()) > float(target): lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    y=np.clip(x-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=float(target - y.sum())
    for _ in range(4):
       if abs(diff) <= 1e-13: break
       mask=(y>1e-12)&(y<1.0-1e-12)
       cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
       if cnt>0: y= y.copy(); y[mask]+=diff/cnt
       else: y = y + diff/n
       y=np.clip(y,0.0,1.0)
       diff=float(target-y.sum())
    return y
```
`y= y.copy()` before mask modify; if y from clip already new? It is new. But ok.

`softmax_values` stable.

`conv_full_pack`:
```
def conv_full_pack(a,z,n):
    m=2*n-1
    if n <= 900:
        return np.convolve(np.asarray(a,dtype=float), np.asarray(z,dtype=complex), mode='full')
    N=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    aa=np.zeros(N, dtype=complex); aa[:n]=a
    bb=np.zeros(N, dtype=complex); bb[:n]=z
    return np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(aa)*np.fft.fft(bb))[:m]
```
If a is h[::-1] view, `np.asarray(a,dtype=float)` maybe copy? okay. But we pass h which is float; z complex. For FFT, `aa[:n]=a` works if a length n; for h[::-1], length n. Good. For direct, `np.convolve` requires 1D. OK.

`smooth_grad_from_w`:
```
def smooth_grad_from_w(h,w,n,dx):
    n=int(n)
    if n==1:
       return (1.0-2.0*h)*w[0]*dx
    mid=n-1
    A=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    B=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    A[1:]=w[mid+1:]
    if mid>0:
       B[1:]=w[:mid][::-1]
    Z=A+1j*B
    back=conv_full_pack(h,Z,n)
    fwd=conv_full_pack(h[::-1],Z,n)
    prefA=np.cumsum(A)
    prefB=np.cumsum(B)
    backr=back[:n]
    fwr=fwd[n-1::-1]
    g = w[mid]*(1.0-2.0*h)
    g = g + (prefA[n-1::-1] - backr.real - fwr.real)
    g = g + (prefB - backr.imag - fwr.imag)
    return g*dx
```
Need `w` length 2n-1. If n=1, mid=0, w[0]. OK.

`compute_smooth`: uses `state['target']`. Ensure state set before. Good.

`s_jac` and `aw_jac` use `np.array_equal`. For complex? no.

`lbfgs_optimize`:
```
def lbfgs_optimize(fun,jac,h,maxiter,deadline):
    x=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    n=int(x.size)
    target=0.5*n
    rem=float(deadline-time.time())
    if rem < 4.0:
        return project_box_sum(x,target)
    mx=int(min(max(maxiter,1), max(12, int(rem/3.0))))
    bounds=[]
    for _ in range(n): bounds.append((0.0,1.0))
    try:
       res=so.minimize(fun,x,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=jac,bounds=bounds,
              options={'maxiter':mx,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-13,'maxls':40,'disp':False})
       y=res.x if getattr(res,'x',None) is not None else x
       return project_box_sum(np.asarray(y,dtype=float),target)
    except Exception:
       return project_box_sum(x,target)
```
`ftol` 1e-16 maybe too low; OK.

`refine_smooth` set rho maybe 1e4. If n large, penalty gradient 1e4*res; maybe. Could set rho = 1e4 * max(1,n/400)? no. Use 1e4.

`make_active_weights`:
```
def make_active_weights(h,n,eps):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')*dx
    C=float(np.max(c))
    active = c > C - float(eps)
    w=np.zeros(len(c), dtype=float)
    floor=0.04
    w.fill(floor/len(w))
    aint=int(np.count_nonzero(active))
    if aint > 0:
       w[active] += (1.0-floor)/aint
    else:
       w[int(np.argmax(c))] += 1.0-floor
    return w
```
If eps very small and active only 1, floor sum 0.04 +0.96=1. Good.

`refine_weighted`: set AW['w']=w (copy? reference ok), target, etc.

`active_polish`:
```
def active_polish(h, deadline, seed):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    n=int(h.size); h=project_box_sum(h,0.5*n)
    best=erdos_c5(h,n)
    eps_list=[1e-2,5e-3,2e-3,1e-3,5e-4,2e-4,1e-4,5e-5]
    mx_base = 35 if n <= 500 else 22
    for eps in eps_list:
       if time.time() > deadline - 12.0: break
       for _ in range(2):
          if time.time() > deadline - 12.0: break
          w=make_active_weights(h,n,eps)
          cand,c=refine_weighted(h,w,mx_base,deadline)
          if c < best - 1e-13:
             h=cand; best=c
    return h,best
```
Maybe 2 per eps *8*25 iter could be long. But time stops. In early phase deadline close, it will do limited? It doesn't use remaining in loop except break; `refine_weighted` caps iterations by rem/3, but if many calls, each can use all remaining? `make_rotation_h`:
```
def make_rotation_h(n, alpha, beta, amp=1.0):
    n=int(n)
    idx=np.arange(n)
    ang=(idx*float(alpha)+float(beta)) % 1.0
    v=0.5 + float(amp)*np.cos(2.0*math.pi*ang)
    v=np.clip(v,0.0,1.0)
    return project_box_sum(v,0.5*n)
```

`continuous_seed_search`: uses `refine_smooth`. Need choose maxiter small.
```
def continuous_seed_search(n, base_h, deadline, seed):
    best_h=np.asarray(base_h,dtype=float); best_c=erdos_c5(best_h,n)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(int(seed)+73)
    for k in (64,96,128,192):
       for beta in (0.18,0.53):
          if time.time()>deadline-20.0: return best_h,best_c
          hs=make_rotation_h(n,1.0/k,beta)
          cand,c=refine_smooth(hs, 700.0, 22, deadline)
          if c<best_c-1e-14: best_h=cand; best_c=c
    for _ in range(6):
       if time.time()>deadline-20.0: break
       k=int(rng.randint(36,220)); beta=float(rng.rand())
       hs=make_rotation_h(n,1.0/k,beta)
       cand,c=refine_smooth(hs, 1500.0, 22, deadline)
       if c<best_c-1e-14: best_h=cand; best_c=c
    return best_h,best_c
```
No lambdas. Good.

`resample_n`:
```
def resample_n(h,n,nh):
    if int(n)==int(nh):
       return project_box_sum(h,0.5*int(nh))
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,int(n))
    y=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,int(nh))
    out=np.interp(y,x,np.asarray(h,dtype=float))
    return project_box_sum(out,0.5*int(nh))
```

Binary helpers:
`bits_from_bool`:
```
def bits_from_bool(arr):
    mask=0
    for i,v in enumerate(arr):
       if v: mask |= (1 << int(i))
    return mask
```
Could be slow for 800 but okay.

`mask_to_array`: loop.

`binary_from_h`:
```
def binary_from_h(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        h=np.interp(np.linspace(0,2,n), np.linspace(0,2,h.size), h)
    m=n//2
    score=-h
    idx=np.argpartition(score, m-1)[:m]
    arr=np.zeros(n,dtype=bool)
    arr[idx]=True
    return bits_from_bool(arr)
```
If m=0? n>=2. If h.size 0? no.

`rotation_binary`:
```
def rotation_binary(n, alpha, beta):
    n=int(n)
    idx=np.arange(n)
    ang=(idx*float(alpha)+float(beta)) % 1.0
    arr=ang < 0.5
    diff=n//2 - int(np.count_nonzero(arr))
    if diff != 0:
       dist=np.empty(n)
       ones=arr
       if np.any(ones):
          dist[ones]=np.minimum(ang[ones], 0.5-ang[ones])
       zeros=~arr
       if np.any(zeros):
          dist[zeros]=np.minimum(1.0-ang[zeros], ang[zeros]-0.5)
       if diff > 0:
          cand=np.flatnonzero(zeros)
       else:
          cand=np.flatnonzero(ones)
       need=abs(diff)
       if cand.size >= need:
          order=np.argsort(dist[cand])[:need]
          if diff > 0: arr[cand[order]]=True
          else: arr[cand[order]]=False
       else:
          arr = bits? If not enough, fallback random? Use choose.
          if diff>0:
             # should not happen unless all ones? If all ones and need ones zero, impossible. But counts differ, enough because density around half and arr count <=n. If all ones and diff<0 enough. If all zeros and diff>0 enough.
          ...
    return bits_from_bool(arr)
```
Since interval length half, arr count should within maybe n, enough. But if alpha weird and count e.g. n (if ang<0.5 all? impossible for beta? If alpha=0? We don't use alpha=0; k finite. But could count diff > cand size? e.g. arr all True, diff=-n/2, cand ones size n, enough. arr all False, cand zeros n. OK.
Need if `dist` unassigned for empty? If ones empty, dist[ones] empty no init; if zeros not empty assigned. If diff>0 and zeros maybe empty? If arr all True and diff negative, not diff>0. OK. But `dist` entries for arr false assigned; if diff>0, zeros exist. Good.
Simpler avoid uninitialized: compute dist all:
```
dist_ones = np.minimum(np.maximum(ang,0.0),0.5)??
```
Let's do robust:
```
dist = np.minimum(np.abs(ang), np.abs(ang-0.5))
dist = np.minimum(dist, np.abs(ang-1.0))
```
But boundaries at 0 and 0.5. For angle in [0,1), distance to boundary 0 or 0.5: `dist1=np.minimum(ang, 1.0-ang)` nearest to 0; `dist2=np.abs(ang-0.5)` nearest to 0.5; `dist=np.minimum(dist1,dist2)`. This works for both ones and zeros. For angle 0.75, dist1=0.25, dist2=0.25 => 0.25. For angle 0.6, dist1=0.4, dist2=0.1 =>0.1. Good. Use that.
```
dist=np.minimum(np.minimum(ang,1.0-ang), np.abs(ang-0.5))
```

`binary_local_search`: Need if `start_mask` sum exact. Our generators do. But random perturb valid swaps.
```
def binary_local_search(start_mask,n,fullmask,masks,deadline,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(int(seed)+211)
    cur_mask=int(start_mask)
    cur_val=int(binary_eval(cur_mask,n,fullmask,masks))
    best_mask=cur_mask; best_val=cur_val
    temp=1.0; stuck=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
       cands=binary_top_pairs(cur_mask,n,fullmask,masks,cur_val,rng,40)
       if not cands: break
       accepted=False
       for ii in range(len(cands)):
           i,j=cands[ii]
           if time.time() > deadline - 2.0: break
           if i<0 or i>=n or j<0 or j>=n or i==j: continue
           if ((cur_mask>>i) ^ (cur_mask>>j)) & 1 == 0: continue
           nm=cur_mask ^ (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
           val=int(binary_eval(nm,n,fullmask,masks))
           if val < cur_val:
              cur_mask=nm; cur_val=val; accepted=True
              if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_mask=nm
              break
           if val <= cur_val + 2:
              if float(rng.rand()) < math.exp(-(float(val)-float(cur_val))/max(temp,1e-6)):
                 cur_mask=nm; cur_val=val; accepted=True
                 break
       if not accepted:
          temp *= 0.985
          stuck += 1
          if stuck > 25:
             k=3 if stuck < 60 else 8
             for _ in range(k):
                found=False
                for _ in range(30):
                   i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
                   if i!=j and (((cur_mask>>i) ^ (cur_mask>>j)) & 1):
                      cur_mask ^= (1<<i) ^ (1<<j); found=True; break
             if found:
                cur_val=int(binary_eval(cur_mask,n,fullmask,masks))
                if cur_val < best_val: best_val=cur_val; best_mask=cur_mask
             temp=1.0; stuck=0
       else:
          stuck=max(0,stuck-2)
          if cur_val > best_val + 8:
             cur_mask=best_mask; cur_val=best_val; temp=1.0; stuck=0
    return best_mask,best_val
```
Bug: In inner perturb, `found` local to for? If any swap done, found True. But if first swap found found=True, second inner may also set. At end, if found reeval. OK. However if no valid swaps found (shouldn't), found remains False. Good.

`if ((cur_mask>>i) ^ (cur_mask>>j)) & 1 == 0` operator precedence: `&` lower than `==`? In Python, bitwise & has lower precedence than ==? Actually comparisons lower than bitwise? Let's be safe: `if (((cur_mask >> i) ^ (cur_mask >> j)) & 1) == 0:`. Similarly perturb.

`binary_run_n`: Need if `n` not even? It should be even; if odd, target half invalid for binary. In run, use even.
```
def binary_run_n(n, base_h, deadline, seed):
    n=int(n)
    if n < 2: ...
    fullmask=(1<<n)-1
    masks=[]
    for d in range(1,n): masks.append((1 << (n-d))-1)
    seeds=generate_binary_seeds(n,base_h,seed)
    scored=[]
    for m in seeds:
       v=binary_eval(m,n,fullmask,masks)
       scored.append((v,m))
    scored.sort()
    best_val=scored[0][0]; best_mask=scored[0][1]
    idx=0; L=len(scored)
    while idx<L and time.time()<deadline-12:
       v,m=scored[idx]
       remaining=L-idx
       share=float(deadline - time.time() - 10.0)/max(1,remaining)
       sub=time.time()+max(6.0,min(60.0,share))
       if sub>deadline-10: sub=deadline-10
       bm,bv=binary_local_search(m,n,fullmask,masks,sub,int(seed)+idx*7+31)
       if bv<best_val: best_val=bv; best_mask=bm
       idx+=1
       if best_val * 2.0 / n <= 0.375:
           break
    h=mask_to_array(best_mask,n)
    c=erdos_c5(h,n)
    return h,c
```
If seeds empty? generate always. If binary_eval very low and time, break.

`generate_binary_seeds`: `base_h` maybe best_h with length different. Need if base_h None? no.
```
def generate_binary_seeds(n, base_h, seed):
    n=int(n)
    h=np.asarray(base_h,dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
       h=resample_n(h,h.size,n)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(int(seed)+5)
    seeds=[]
    seeds.append(binary_from_h(h,n))
    for k in (48,72,96,128,192):
       for beta in (0.23,0.61):
          seeds.append(rotation_binary(n,1.0/float(k),float(beta)))
    for _ in range(5):
       k=int(rng.randint(24,220))
       beta=float(rng.rand())
       seeds.append(rotation_binary(n,1.0/float(k),float(beta)))
    for _ in range(3):
       idx=rng.choice(n,size=n//2,replace=False)
       arr=np.zeros(n,dtype=bool); arr[idx]=True
       seeds.append(bits_from_bool(arr))
    seen=set(); uniq=[]
    for m in seeds:
       if m not in seen:
          seen.add(m); uniq.append(m)
    return uniq
```
Seeds count 1+10+5+3=19.

Can simplify `rotation_binary`: use threshold and argpartition top n/2 of a score vector tuned to boundaries. We can define continuous score `score = np.cos(2π ang?)` not exactly interval. But for binary seed, any top n/2 pattern; we can use `score = -(np.minimum(ang,0.5-ang).clip?)` no. We can generate exact balanced by sorting distance: Bit 1 if `ang < 0.5`; if count off adjust by sorting dist. That's fine. `rotation_binary` can be compact:
```
def rotation_binary(n,alpha,beta):
 idx=np.arange(n); ang=(idx*alpha+beta)%1.0
 arr=ang<0.5; diff=n//2-arr.sum()
 if diff:
  dist=np.minimum(np.minimum(ang,1-ang),np.abs(ang-0.5))
  cand=np.flatnonzero(~arr if diff>0 else arr)
  order=np.argsort(dist[cand])[:abs(diff)]
  arr[cand[order]]=diff>0
 return bits_from_bool(arr)
```
This assumes cand enough; OK. 8 lines.

def bits_from_bool(a):

    for i,v in enumerate(a):
        if v: m|=1<<i
    return m

```python
import time, math
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as so
OPT={'n':0,'dx':1,'target':0,'beta':1,'rho':1e4,'w':None,'cx':None,'cg':None}
def erdos_c5(h,n): return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*(2.0/n))
def project_box_sum(x,t):
 x=np.asarray(x,float); n=x.size
 if n==0: return x
 y=np.clip(x,0,1)
 if abs(y.sum()-t)<1e-13: return y
 lo=float(np.min(x))-1; hi=float(np.max(x))+1
 for _ in range(70):
  m=0.5*(lo+hi); yy=np.clip(x-m,0,1)
  if float(yy.sum())>t: lo=m
  else: hi=m
 y=np.clip(x-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1); d=t-y.sum()
 for _ in range(4):
  if abs(d)<1e-13: break
  m=(y>1e-12)&(y<1-1e-12); c=m.sum()
  if c: y=y.copy(); y[m]+=d/c
  else: y=y+d/n
  y=np.clip(y,0,1); d=t-y.sum()
 return y
```

Detailed compact functions:

`smooth_grad`:
```
def smooth_grad(h,w,n,dx):
 mid=n-1; A=np.zeros(n); B=np.zeros(n)
 A[1:]=w[mid+1:]
 if mid: B[1:]=w[:mid][::-1]
 Z=A+1j*B; back=conv_pack(h,Z,n); fwd=conv_pack(h[::-1],Z,n)
 pa=np.cumsum(A); pb=np.cumsum(B); br=back[:n]; fr=fwd[n-1::-1]
 g=w[mid]*(1-2*h)+(pa[n-1::-1]-br.real-fr.real)+(pb-br.imag-fr.imag)
 return g*dx
```
If n=1, A[1:] empty, mid=0, conv h length1 ok. Works? `fwd[n-1::-1]` with n=1 is fwd[0::-1] -> index0 only. pa[0::-1] ok. B empty. Good. No special needed. But for n=1, `w[mid+1:]` length0; `w[:mid]` length0. OK.

`opt_compute`:
```
def opt_compute(x):
 st=OPT; n=st['n']; dx=st['dx']; w=st['w']
 c=np.correlate(x,1-x,mode='full')*dx
 if w is None:
  f,tp=softmax_values(c,st['beta']); g=smooth_grad(x,tp,n,dx)
 else:
  f=float(np.dot(w,c)); g=smooth_grad(x,w,n,dx)
 res=float(x.sum()-st['target']); rho=st['rho']
 if rho: f+=0.5*rho*res*res; g=g+rho*res
 st['cx']=x.copy(); st['cg']=g
 return f
```
No store f.

`opt_jac`:
```
def opt_jac(x):
 st=OPT
 if st['cx'] is not None and np.array_equal(x,st['cx']): return st['cg'].copy()
 opt_compute(x); return st['cg'].copy()
```

`optimize_lbfgs`:
```
def optimize_lbfgs(f,j,x,mx,dl):
 x=np.asarray(x,float); n=x.size; t=0.5*n
 rem=dl-time.time()
 if rem<3: return project_box_sum(x,t)
 mx=int(min(mx,max(3,int(rem/2.0))))
 b=[(0,1)]*n
 try:
  r=so.minimize(f,x,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=j,bounds=b,options={'maxiter':mx,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-13,'maxls':40})
  return project_box_sum(np.asarray(r.x,float),t)
 except Exception: return project_box_sum(x,t)
```

`refine_opt`:
```
def refine_opt(x,mx,dl,beta=None,w=None):
 n=len(x); OPT['n']=n; OPT['dx']=2.0/n; OPT['target']=0.5*n; OPT['rho']=1e4
 if beta is not None: OPT['beta']=float(beta); OPT['w']=None
 else: OPT['w']=w
 OPT['cx']=None; OPT['cg']=None
 c=optimize_lbfgs(opt_fun,opt_jac,x,mx,dl)
 return c, erdos_c5(c,n)
```
Need `w` array maybe length? yes.

`active_w`:
```
def active_w(h,n,eps):
 c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')*(2.0/n); C=c.max()
 w=np.full(len(c),0.04/len(c)); a=c>=C-eps; m=int(a.sum())
 if m: w[a]+=0.96/m
 else: w[int(np.argmax(c))]+=0.96
 return w
```

`active_polish`:
```
def active_polish(h,dl):
 h=project_box_sum(np.asarray(h,float),0.5*len(h)); n=len(h); b=erdos_c5(h,n)
 mx=30 if n<=500 else 18
 for e in (1e-2,5e-3,2e-3,1e-3,5e-4,2e-4,1e-4):
  if time.time()>dl-10: break
  w=active_w(h,n,e)
  cc,c=refine_opt(h,mx,dl,w=w)
  if c<b-1e-13: h=cc; b=c
 return h,b
```

`resample`:
```
def resample(h,n,m):
 if n==m: return project_box_sum(h,0.5*n)
 x=np.linspace(0,2,n); y=np.interp(np.linspace(0,2,m),x,h)
 return project_box_sum(y,0.5*m)
```

Binary:
`bits`:
```
def bits(a):
 m=0
 for i,v in enumerate(a):
  if v: m|=1<<i
 return m
```

`mask_arr`:
```
def mask_arr(m,n):
 a=np.zeros(n)
 for i in range(n):
  if (m>>i)&1: a[i]=1
 return a
```

`bin_from_h`:
```
def bin_from_h(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float)
 if h.size!=n:
  h=np.interp(np.linspace(0,2,n),np.linspace(0,2,h.size),h)
 u=np.argpartition(-h,n//2-1)[:n//2]
 a=np.zeros(n,bool); a[u]=1
 return bits(a)
```
If n//2-1 negative? n>=2, m>=1. For n=2, m-1=0 OK. If n=0 no.

`rot_bin`:
```
def rot_bin(n,al,be):
 i=np.arange(n); ang=(i*al+be)%1.0
 a=ang<0.5; d=n//2-int(a.sum())
 if d:
  dist=np.minimum(np.minimum(ang,1-ang),np.abs(ang-0.5))
  cand=np.flatnonzero(~a if d>0 else a)
  order=np.argsort(dist[cand])[:abs(d)]
  a[cand[order]]=d>0
 return bits(a)
```
Need `a[cand[order]]=d>0`: if d>0 True, else False. Works.

`gen_seeds`:
```
def gen_seeds(n,base,seed):
 h=base
 if len(h)!=n: h=resample(h,len(h),n)
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 s=[bin_from_h(h,n)]
 for k in (48,72,96,128,192):
  for b in (0.23,0.61): s.append(rot_bin(n,1.0/k,b))
 for _ in range(5):
  k=int(rng.randint(24,220)); s.append(rot_bin(n,1.0/k,float(rng.rand())))
 for _ in range(2):
  u=rng.choice(n,n//2,replace=False); a=np.zeros(n,bool); a[u]=1; s.append(bits(a))
 out=[]; seen=set()
 for m in s:
  if m not in seen: seen.add(m); out.append(m)
 return out
```
List literal `s` with dynamic appends; okay.

`eval_bits`:
```
def eval_bits(m,n,full,masks):
 z=full^m; b=0
 for d in range(1,n):
  s=m>>d; ma=masks[d-1]
  a=(m & (ma ^ s)).bit_count()
  c=(z & s & ma).bit_count()
  if a>b: b=a
  if c>b: b=c
 return b
```

`top_pairs`:
```
def top_pairs(m,n,full,masks,best,rng,mp):
 z=full^m; top=[]
 for d in range(1,n):
  s=m>>d; ma=masks[d-1]
  a=(m & (ma ^ s)).bit_count()
  c=(z & s & ma).bit_count()
  if a>=best-2 and a: top.append((a,d,m & (ma ^ s) & ma))
  if c>=best-2 and c: top.append((c,d,z & s & ma))
 if not top: return random_pairs(m,n,rng,mp)
 top.sort(reverse=True); pairs=[]
 for _,d,bad in top[:8]:
  x=bad
  while x and len(pairs)<mp:
   lsb=x&-x; i=lsb.bit_length()-1; j=i+d
   if j<n: pairs.append((i,j))
   x^=lsb
 pairs+=random_pairs(m,n,rng,max(8,mp//3))
 return list(dict.fromkeys(pairs))[:mp]
```

def random_pairs(m,n,rng,k):
 out=[]; att=0
 while len(out)<k and att<k*6:
  att+=1; i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
  if i!=j and (((m>>i)^(m>>j))&1): out.append((i,j))
 return out

`local_bin`:
```
def local_bin(start,n,full,masks,dl,seed):
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed); cur=start; val=eval_bits(cur,n,full,masks)
 best_val=val; best_mask=cur; T=1.0; stuck=0
 while time.time()<dl-2:
  cands=top_pairs(cur,n,full,masks,val,rng,50)
  acc=False
  for i,j in cands:
   if time.time()>dl-2: break
   if i<0 or i>=n or j<0 or j>=n or i==j: continue
   if not(((cur>>i)^(cur>>j))&1): continue
   nm=cur^(1<<i)^(1<<j); nv=eval_bits(nm,n,full,masks)
   if nv<val:
    cur=nm; val=nv; acc=True
    if nv<best_val: best_val=nv; best_mask=nm
    break
   if nv<=val+2 and rng.rand()<math.exp(-(nv-val)/max(T,1e-6)):
    cur=nm; val=nv; acc=True
    if nv<best_val: best_val=nv; best_mask=nm
    break
  if not acc:
   T*=0.985; stuck+=1
   if stuck>25:
    k=3 if stuck<60 else 8
    for _ in range(k):
     pr=random_pairs(cur,n,rng,1)
     if not pr: break
     i,j=pr[0]; cur^=(1<<i)^(1<<j)
    val=eval_bits(cur,n,full,masks)
    if val<best_val: best_val=val; best_mask=cur
    T=1.0; stuck=0
  else:
   stuck=max(0,stuck-2)
   if val>best_val+8: cur=best_mask; val=best_val; T=1.0; stuck=0
 return best_mask,best_val
```
About 35.

def binary_run(n,base,dl,seed):
 full=(1<<n)-1; masks=[(1<<(n-d))-1 for d in range(1,n)]
 seeds=gen_seeds(n,base,seed); sc=[]
 for m in seeds: sc.append((eval_bits(m,n,full,masks),m))
 sc.sort(); bv, bm=sc[0]
 i=0; L=len(sc)
 while i<L and time.time()<dl-12:
  v,m=sc[i]; rem=L-i
  sub=time.time()+max(6,min(50,(dl-time.time()-10)/rem))
  if sub>dl-10: sub=dl-10
  mm,val=local_bin(m,n,full,masks,sub,seed+i*7+1)
  if val<bv: bv=val; bm=mm
  i+=1
  if bv*2.0/n<=0.375: break
 h=mask_arr(bm,n); return h,erdos_c5(h,n)

```python
import time, math
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as so

OPT = {'n': 0, 'dx': 1.0, 'target': 0.0, 'beta': 1.0, 'rho': 1e4, 'w': None, 'cx': None, 'cg': None}

def erdos_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project_box_sum(x, t):
    x = np.asarray(x, float)
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return x
    y = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(y.sum()) - float(t)) < 1e-13:
        return y
    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        yy = np.clip(x - m, 0.0, 1.0)
        if float(yy.sum()) > float(t):
            lo = m
        else:
            hi = m
    y = np.clip(x - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = float(t - y.sum())
    for _ in range(4):
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        m = (y > 1e-12) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        c = int(np.count_nonzero(m))
        if c:
            y = y.copy()
            y[m] += d / c
        else:
            y = y + d / n
        y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
        d = float(t - y.sum())
    return y

def softmax_values(c, beta):
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    z = beta * (c - mc)
    np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0, out=z)
    ew = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ew.sum())
    f = mc + math.log(s) / beta if s > 0.0 else mc
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = ew / s
    return f, w

def conv_pack(a, z, n):
    return np.convolve(np.asarray(a, float), np.asarray(z, complex), mode='full')

def smooth_grad(h, w, n, dx):
    mid = n - 1
    A = np.zeros(n, float)
    B = np.zeros(n, float)
    A[1:] = w[mid + 1:]
    if mid:
        B[1:] = w[:mid][::-1]
    Z = A + 1j * B
    back = conv_pack(h, Z, n)
    fwd = conv_pack(h[::-1], Z, n)
    pa = np.cumsum(A)
    pb = np.cumsum(B)
    br = back[:n]
    fr = fwd[n - 1::-1]
    g = w[mid] * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    g = g + (pa[n - 1::-1] - br.real - fr.real)
    g = g + (pb - br.imag - fr.imag)
    return g * dx

def opt_compute(x):
    st = OPT
    n = int(st['n'])
    dx = float(st['dx'])
    w = st['w']
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full') * dx
    if w is None:
        f, tw = softmax_values(c, float(st['beta']))
        g = smooth_grad(x, tw, n, dx)
    else:
        f = float(np.dot(w, c))
        g = smooth_grad(x, w, n, dx)
    res = float(x.sum() - st['target'])
    rho = float(st['rho'])
    if rho:
        f += 0.5 * rho * res * res
        g = g + rho * res
    st['cx'] = x.copy()
    st['cg'] = g
    return f

def opt_fun(x):
    return opt_compute(x)

def opt_jac(x):
    st = OPT
    if st['cx'] is not None and np.array_equal(x, st['cx']):
        return st['cg'].copy()
    opt_compute(x)
    return st['cg'].copy()

def optimize_lbfgs(f, j, x, mx, dl):
    x = np.asarray(x, float)
    n = int(x.size)
    t = 0.5 * n
    rem = float(dl - time.time())
    if rem < 3.0:
        return project_box_sum(x, t)
    mx = int(min(mx, max(3, int(rem / 2.0))))
    b = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        r = so.minimize(f, x, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=j, bounds=b,
                        options={'maxiter': mx, 'ftol': 1e-16, 'gtol': 1e-13, 'maxls': 40, 'disp': 0})
        return project_box_sum(np.asarray(r.x, float), t)
    except Exception:
        return project_box_sum(x, t)

def refine_opt(x, mx, dl, beta=None, w=None):
    x = np.asarray(x, float)
    n = int(x.size)
    OPT['n'] = n
    OPT['dx'] = 2.0 / n
    OPT['target'] = 0.5 * n
    OPT['rho'] = 1e4
    if beta is not None:
        OPT['beta'] = float(beta)
        OPT['w'] = None
    else:
        OPT['w'] = w
    OPT['cx'] = None
    OPT['cg'] = None
    c = optimize_lbfgs(opt_fun, opt_jac, x, mx, dl)
    return c, erdos_c5(c, n)

def active_w(h, n, eps):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * (2.0 / n)
    C = float(np.max(c))
    w = np.full(len(c), 0.04 / len(c))
    a = c >= (C - float(eps))
    m = int(np.count_nonzero(a))
    if m:
        w[a] += 0.96 / m
    else:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] += 0.96
    return w

def active_polish(h, dl):
    h = project_box_sum(np.asarray(h, float), 0.5 * len(h))
    n = int(h.size)
    b = erdos_c5(h, n)
    mx = 30 if n <= 500 else 18
    for e in (1e-2, 5e-3, 2e-3, 1e-3, 5e-4, 2e-4, 1e-4):
        if time.time() > dl - 10.0:
            break
        w = active_w(h, n, e)
        cc, c = refine_opt(h, mx, dl, w=w)
        if c < b - 1e-13:
            h, b = cc, c
    return h, b

def resample(h, n, m):
    if int(n) == int(m):
        return project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * int(n))
    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(n))
    fp = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(m)), xp, np.asarray(h, float))
    return project_box_sum(fp, 0.5 * int(m))

def bits(a):
    m = 0
    for i, v in enumerate(a):
        if v:
            m |= 1 << int(i)
    return m

def mask_arr(m, n):
    a = np.zeros(int(n), float)
    for i in range(int(n)):
        if (m >> i) & 1:
            a[i] = 1.0
    return a

def bin_from_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n), np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, h.size), h)
    k = n // 2
    u = np.argpartition(-h, k - 1)[:k]
    a = np.zeros(n, bool)
    a[u] = True
    return bits(a)

def rot_bin(n, al, be):
    i = np.arange(n)
    ang = (i * float(al) + float(be)) % 1.0
    a = ang < 0.5
    d = n // 2 - int(np.count_nonzero(a))
    if d:
        dist = np.minimum(np.minimum(ang, 1.0 - ang), np.abs(ang - 0.5))
        cand = np.flatnonzero(~a if d > 0 else a)
        order = np.argsort(dist[cand])[:abs(d)]
        a[cand[order]] = d > 0
    return bits(a)

def gen_seeds(n, base, seed):
    h = np.asarray(base, float)
    if h.size != n:
        h = resample(h, h.size, n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    s = [bin_from_h(h, n)]
    for k in (48, 72, 96, 128, 192):
        for b in (0.23, 0.61):
            s.append(rot_bin(n, 1.0 / float(k), float(b)))
    for _ in range(5):
        k = int(rng.randint(24, 220))
        s.append(rot_bin(n, 1.0 / float(k), float(rng.rand())))
    for _ in range(2):
        u = rng.choice(n, n // 2, replace=False)
        a = np.zeros(n, bool)
        a[u] = True
        s.append(bits(a))
    out = []
    seen = set()
    for m in s:
        if m not in seen:
            seen.add(m)
            out.append(m)
    return out

def eval_bits(m, n, full, masks):
    z = full ^ m
    b = 0
    for d in range(1, n):
        s = m >> d
        ma = masks[d - 1]
        a = (m & (ma ^ s)).bit_count()
        c = (z & s & ma).bit_count()
        if a > b:
            b = a
        if c > b:
            b = c
    return b

def random_pairs(m, n, rng, k):
    out = []
    att = 0
    while len(out) < k and att < k * 6:
        att += 1
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i != j and (((m >> i) ^ (m >> j)) & 1):
            out.append((i, j))
    return out

def top_pairs(m, n, full, masks, best, rng, mp):
    z = full ^ m
    top = []
    for d in range(1, n):
        s = m >> d
        ma = masks[d - 1]
        a = (m & (ma ^ s)).bit_count()
        c = (z & s & ma).bit_count()
        if a >= best - 2 and a:
            top.append((a, d, m & (ma ^ s) & ma))
        if c >= best - 2 and c:
            top.append((c, d, z & s & ma))
    if not top:
        return random_pairs(m, n, rng, mp)
    top.sort(reverse=True)
    pairs = []
    for _, d, bad in top[:8]:
        x = bad
        while x and len(pairs) < mp:
            lsb = x & -x
            i = lsb.bit_length() - 1
            j = i + d
            if j < n:
                pairs.append((i, j))
            x ^= lsb
    pairs += random_pairs(m, n, rng, max(8, mp // 3))
    return list(dict.fromkeys(pairs))[:mp]

def local_bin(start, n, full, masks, dl, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    cur = int(start)
    val = eval_bits(cur, n, full, masks)
    best_val = val
    best_mask = cur
    T = 1.0
    stuck = 0
    while time.time() < dl - 2.0:
        cands = top_pairs(cur, n, full, masks, val, rng, 50)
        acc = False
        for i, j in cands:
            if time.time() > dl - 2.0:
                break
            if i < 0 or i >= n or j < 0 or j >= n or i == j:
                continue
            if not (((cur >> i) ^ (cur >> j)) & 1):
                continue
            nm = cur ^ (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
            nv = eval_bits(nm, n, full, masks)
            if nv < val:
                cur, val, acc = nm, nv, True
                if nv < best_val:
                    best_val, best_mask = nv, nm
                break
            if nv <= val + 2 and float(rng.rand()) < math.exp(-(float(nv) - float(val)) / max(T, 1e-6)):
                cur, val, acc = nm, nv, True
                if nv < best_val:
                    best_val, best_mask = nv, nm
                break
        if not acc:
            T *= 0.985
            stuck += 1
            if stuck > 25:
                k = 3 if stuck < 60 else 8
                for _ in range(k):
                    pr = random_pairs(cur, n, rng, 1)
                    if not pr:
                        break
                    i, j = pr[0]
                    cur ^= (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
                val = eval_bits(cur, n, full, masks)
                if val < best_val:
                    best_val, best_mask = val, cur
                T, stuck = 1.0, 0
        else:
            stuck = max(0, stuck - 2)
            if val > best_val + 8:
                cur, val, T, stuck = best_mask, best_val, 1.0, 0
    return best_mask, best_val

def binary_run(n, base, dl, seed):
    n = int(n)
    full = (1 << n) - 1
    masks = [(1 << (n - d)) - 1 for d in range(1, n)]
    seeds = gen_seeds(n, base, seed)
    sc = []
    for m in seeds:
        sc.append((eval_bits(m, n, full, masks), m))
    sc.sort()
    if sc[0][0] * 2.0 / n <= 0.3801:
        h = mask_arr(sc[0][1], n)
        return h, erdos_c5(h, n)
    bv, bm = sc[0]
    i = 0
    L = len(sc)
    while i < L and time.time() < dl - 12.0:
        v, m = sc[i]
        rem = L - i
        sub = time.time() + max(6.0, min(50.0, (dl - time.time() - 10.0) / max(1, rem)))
        if sub > dl - 10.0:
            sub = dl - 10.0
        mm, val = local_bin(m, n, full, masks, sub, int(seed) + i * 7 + 1)
        if val < bv:
            bv, bm = val, mm
        i += 1
        if bv * 2.0 / n <= 0.3801:
            break
    h = mask_arr(bm, n)
    return h, erdos_c5(h, n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    dl = t0 + total
    try:
        h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, float).copy()
        n = int(h.size)
    except Exception:
        n = 400
        a = np.arange(n)
        h = 0.5 + np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * a / 80.0 + 0.2)
    if n < 20:
        n = 400
        a = np.arange(n)
        h = 0.5 + np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * a / 80.0 + 0.2)
    if n > 600:
        h = resample(h, n, 600)
        n = 600
    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
    bh, bn, bc = h, n, erdos_c5(h, n)
    e1 = t0 + 0.14 * total
    for b, mx in ((100, 60), (1000, 60), (10000, 50), (100000, 40)):
        if time.time() > e1 - 5.0:
            break
        cc, c = refine_opt(bh, mx, e1, beta=b)
        if c < bc - 1e-14:
            bh, bc = cc.c if False else cc, c
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    for sgm in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10):
        if time.time() > e1 - 8.0:
            break
        hc = project_box_sum(bh + sgm * rng.randn(bn), 0.5 * bn)
        cc, c = refine_opt(hc, 25, e1, beta=20000)
        if c < bc - 1e-14:
            bh, bc = cc, c
    if time.time() < e1:
        bh, bc = active_polish(bh, e1)
    e2 = t0 + 0.60 * total
    if time.time() < e2 - 40.0:
        nb = bn if bn % 2 == 0 else bn - 1
        if nb >= 60:
            base = bh if bn == nb else resample(bh, bn, nb)
            hb, c = binary_run(nb, base, e2, int(seed) + 13)
            if c < bc - 1e-15:
                bh, bn, bc = hb, nb, c
    e3 = t0 + 0.75 * total
    if time.time() < e3 - 20.0:
        for b, mx in ((3000, 40), (30000, 35), (300000, 25)):
            if time.time() > e3 - 15.0:
                break
            cc, c = refine_opt(bh, mx, e3, beta=b)
            if c < bc - 1e-14:
                bh, bc = cc, c
        if time.time() < e3:
            bh, bc = active_polish(bh, e3)
    e4 = dl - 10.0
    if time.time() < e4 - 80.0 and bn != 300:
        base300 = resample(bh, bn, 300)
        sub = min(e4 - 20.0, time.time() + 0.55 * max(1.0, e4 - time.time()))
        hb, c = binary_run(300, base300, sub, int(seed) + 300)
        if c < bc - 1e-15:
            bh, bn, bc = hb, 300, c
        if time.time() < e4 - 20.0:
            src = bh if bn == 300 else base300
            cc, c = refine_opt(src, 30, e4, beta=30000)
            if c < bc - 1e-15:
                bh, bn, bc = cc, 300, c
    if time.time() < dl - 25.0:
        cc, c = refine_opt(bh, 30, dl - 5.0, beta=500000)
        if c < bc - 1e-14:
            bh, bc = cc, c
    if time.time() < dl - 10.0:
        bh, bc = active_polish(bh, dl - 5.0)
    bh = project_box_sum(bh, 0.5 * bn)
    bc = erdos_c5(bh, bn)
    return bh, float(bc), int(bn)
```
